Doses are now recorded as written and replayed after an INTERNAL reset; a `reset` the USER types drops the record, because that is what `reset` means. **`aging` ACCEPTED TARGETS IT COULD NOT AGE TO**: `aging nan` wrote NaN doses and reported them aged (every comparison with NaN is false, so `t <= 0` let it through), `aging abc` aged to 0 s, `dynamic verbose` ate `verbose` as the stop time then blamed `agerate` -- **E-497**'s shape. **A NaN SPEC BOUND IS NEVER VIOLATED**, so it is identical to omitting it: `montecarlo -max nan` reported 100% yield with a Wilson interval. `-max`/`-min`/`-tol`/`-step`/`-scale` now parsed across all four statistical commands -- with `ft_numparse`, NOT `strtod`: the first guard refused `dynamic 20u`, E-157's own documented spelling, and the existing `aging` suite caught it. A NEGATIVE bound stays legal, which is why these are not **E-499**'s `-tol` reader. **A YIELD FROM SAMPLES THAT NEVER VARIED** is one point measured N times; **E-495** warns per `agauss` call, this is the whole-run version. **`optimize` PUBLISHED THE SCORE BUT NOT THE ANSWER** -- `dcenter_yield`/`dcenter_cpk` but never the centred knob, so the shipped dcenter demo printed *checkvalid: vector xc is not available* on every run since it shipped; now `optimize_<name>`. **`aging 1e8 param w`** wrote 4.095e8 into a MOSFET WIDTH (409,500 km), reported 1 device aged, rc=0 -- warns and proceeds when the device also has an `age`. Verified `agestate` **30/30** both solvers, **19 fail pre-fix**, regression **415/415**. **No openvaf-r change.** |
| [E-502](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-502.md) | src/frontend/parser/numparse.c, src/include/ngspice/fteext.h, src/frontend/com_envelope.c, src/frontend/com_emir.c, src/frontend/com_eye.c, src/frontend/com_qpss.c, src/frontend/com_reduce.c, src/frontend/com_hbosc.c, src/frontend/com_compose.c, src/frontend/com_rfstab.c, examples/nanguard_examples/ (new) | **THE GUARD THAT REFUSES THE WRONG VALUE AND ADMITS NaN.** Six report-producing commands wrote `if (x <= 0.0) refuse;`; every comparison with NaN is false, so that refuses 0, refuses a negative number, and lets **NaN straight through**. **`envelope <node> nan <tstop>` SIGSEGVd 5/5** -- `T = 1/fc` NaN, internal `tran nan nan 0 nan` REFUSED, refusal never checked, `EFanalysis` derefs a matrix never built (`SMPmatSize`, 0x10). Both halves fixed: the argument, and the unchecked internal run that **any** refused transient could trigger (**E-438**'s shape). **A LIMIT NEVER EXCEEDED**: `emir jmax nan` = *0 segments over Jmax*, all `ok`, on a grid with 2 genuine violations; `reduce nan` = *26 nodes -> 26 nodes (1.0x)* and a `reduced.sp` that reduced nothing; `qpss v(o) nan 1.1e6` = a 25-row all-`nan` spectrum; `eye -ui nan` = **eye height 0**, a fully closed link -- **E-501**'s NaN spec bound one command over. **TWO RETURNED A DIFFERENT ANSWER, NOT A BLANK**: `eye -tstart nan` never skipped (the skip test is a comparison too) so the startup transient was folded in for **660x the RMS jitter**; `qpss maxorder nan` silently became `order <= 1`, dropping every intermodulation product. Both clamped through an **undefined** double->int conversion, as did `compose lin=1e12` -- INT_MAX, **17 GB really allocated**. **`emir` COULD NOT TELL A GIVEN WIDTH FROM THE DEFAULT**: `@r[w]` answers 1e-5 for an undimensioned wire, so the likely oversight was analysed as a 10 um conductor and reported `ok`, while `w=0`/`w=-0.5u` were skipped and mislabelled *no width given*; now `RESwidthGiven`, two distinct messages. **The idiom was already in the tree** (`hbosc` `K >= 1`, `compose`'s per-parameter NaN refusal, `stb` delegating to `ac`); guards now share `ft_argpos`/`ft_argfinite`/`ft_argcount` in `numparse.c`, parsing with `ft_numparse` so `thick 0.5u` and `1meg` keep working. Verified `nanguard` **34/34** both solvers, **23 fail pre-fix**, regression **416/416**. **No openvaf-r change.** |
| [E-503](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-503.md) | src/spicelib/analysis/cktdojob.c, src/include/ngspice/cktdefs.h, src/frontend/com_sweep.c, src/frontend/com_sweep.h, examples/reusedev_examples/ (new) | **ONE TRANSISTOR NO LONGER DISABLES SETUP REUSE FOR THE WHOLE CIRCUIT.** **E-471** offered reuse only to circuits built entirely from types whose topology is fixed UNCONDITIONALLY (linear elements, sources) plus OSDI, so a single BJT in an otherwise linear deck cost **3.46x at 300 sections, 10.09x at 1200**. A per-TYPE gate for a per-PARAMETER hazard: a built-in semiconductor decides its collapse in `DEVsetup` from a knowable set and nothing else -- BJT `rc`/`rb`/`re`/**`rco`**, diode `rs`/`rsw`/`vp`/`tt`, JFET `rd`/`rs`, Mos1/2/3/6/9 `rd`/`rs`/`rsh`/`nrd`/`nrs` -- each read off the device's own setup by auditing EVERY `CKTmkVolt` and its guard, not from the parameter docs, which do not say which parameters build nodes (`rco` gates a FOURTH BJT node via its `Given` flag; missed on the first pass). The sweep now DECLARES what it varies: **2.28x / 3.25x / 6.69x** recovered. **THE ABSENCE OF A DECLARATION KEEPS IT SAFE** -- `sw_request_reuse()` CLEARS it, so montecarlo and any `.param` knob inherit nothing and keep the strict gate; whole-token match, so `rsh` is not `rs`. **The declaration is a file-static, NOT a `CKTcircuit` field**: in the struct it grew `CKTcircuit` 256 bytes and made `argguard`'s warn_physics count random -- **PADDING ALONE reproduces that**, so a stale-layout read exists elsewhere and is recorded as an OPEN finding rather than made reachable here. Verified `reusedev` **16/16** both solvers, **8 fail pre-fix**, every reuse check BIT-IDENTICAL against `reusesetup=0` after asserting the curve moves. Regression **417/417**. **No openvaf-r change.** |
| [E-504](../../enhancements_doc/Enhancement-504.md) | src/osdi/osditrunc.c, src/osdi/osdidefs.h, examples/rtdomain_examples/ (new) | **A MODEL MAY NOT DEMAND AN UNBOUNDED STEP COUNT.** Two ways to hang the transient >150 s with no output, no error, no *timestep too small*. `$bound_step(1e-18)` is a perfectly LEGAL positive request taken literally -- that check compares against `CKTdelmin` (~5e-20 for a 12 ns analysis), far BELOW what was asked, so nothing fired: the step was not too small for the solver, it was too small to FINISH. No clamp makes 1.2e10 steps work, so the rule is the only one that bounds the run -- a model may force at most **10^6 steps** across the analysis window, then it is clamped and the instance NAMED ONCE. `$discontinuity(0)` outside any conditional pinned the step to the last accepted delta so it could never grow (**E-55** already made the RETRY edge-triggered for this reason; the CAP needed the same floor); `$discontinuity(-1)` means *no discontinuity*, is fine, stays silent. The compiler-side halves are in the openvaf-r report. **The noise sign is deliberately NOT fixed here**: by `osdinoise.c` the contribution factor is folded in as `fac*|fac|` and **E-42** uses that sign for COHERENT summation. Verified `rtdomain` **16/16** both solvers, **11 fail pre-fix**, regression **418/418**. |
